{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011Cf1LiVkrMBcTm8MgDAWpG","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018wS891cWWLXvtxdRMdbSoz","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/stencil3d_claude-opus-5-cc-medium_omp_r5/stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp","content":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdint>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <omp.h>\n#include <sys/mman.h>\n#include <unistd.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nusing Real = double;\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Drop the physical pages backing a freshly allocated (zero-initialized) buffer.\n// The contents stay all-zero, but the pages are re-faulted by whichever thread\n// touches them first, so the parallel initialization below determines the NUMA\n// placement of the grids instead of the thread that ran the allocation.\nvoid releasePages(std::vector<Real>& grid) {\n    const size_t pageSize = static_cast<size_t>(sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE));\n    const uintptr_t begin = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(grid.data());\n    const uintptr_t end = begin + grid.size() * sizeof(Real);\n    const uintptr_t alignedBegin = (begin + pageSize - 1) & ~static_cast<uintptr_t>(pageSize - 1);\n    const uintptr_t alignedEnd = end & ~static_cast<uintptr_t>(pageSize - 1);\n    if (alignedEnd > alignedBegin) {\n        madvise(reinterpret_cast<void*>(alignedBegin), alignedEnd - alignedBegin, MADV_DONTNEED);\n    }\n}\n\n// Parallel first touch + initialization. Must be called from inside a parallel\n// region; the static schedule over z matches the one used by the stencil, so\n// every thread initializes exactly the slices it will later compute on.\nvoid initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t base = z * slice + y * nx;\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = base + x;\n                g[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Parallel first touch of the second buffer, using the same distribution.\nvoid touchGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        std::fill_n(g + z * slice, slice, Real(0));\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation. Called from inside a parallel region; the\n// `omp for` provides the barrier that separates consecutive iterations.\nvoid stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input,\n                      std::vector<Real>& output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const Real* __restrict const in = input.data();\n    Real* __restrict const out = output.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n    // A single pass over the grid: interior points get the stencil, boundary\n    // points are copied (same result as the original two-pass formulation).\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1) {\n            // Entire slice is boundary\n            std::memcpy(out + z * slice, in + z * slice, slice * sizeof(Real));\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // y = 0 and y = ny-1 rows are boundary\n        std::memcpy(out + z * slice, in + z * slice, nx * sizeof(Real));\n        std::memcpy(out + z * slice + (ny - 1) * nx, in + z * slice + (ny - 1) * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));\n\n        for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t base = z * slice + y * nx;\n            const Real* __restrict const center = in + base;\n            const Real* __restrict const front = in + base - nx;\n            const Real* __restrict const back = in + base + nx;\n            const Real* __restrict const bottom = in + base - slice;\n            const Real* __restrict const top = in + base + slice;\n            Real* __restrict const dst = out + base;\n\n            dst[0] = center[0];  // x = 0 boundary\n\n#pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t x = 1; x + 1 < nx; ++x) {\n                dst[x] = (center[x] + center[x - 1] + center[x + 1] + front[x] + back[x] + bottom[x] + top[x]) / 7.0;\n            }\n\n            dst[nx - 1] = center[nx - 1];  // x = nx-1 boundary\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    const Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t n = grid.size();\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    bool badValue = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|| : badValue)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(g[i]) || std::isinf(g[i])) {\n            badValue = true;\n        }\n    }\n    if (badValue) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate grids (double buffering)\n Must be called from inside a parallel\n// region; the static schedule over z matches the one used by the stencil, so\n// every thread initializes exactly the slices it will later compute on.\nvoid initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t base = z * slice + y * nx;\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = base + x;\n                g[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Parallel first touch of the second buffer, using the same distribution.\nvoid touchGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        std::fill_n(g + z * slice, slice, Real(0));\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation. Called from inside a parallel region; the\n// `omp for` provides the barrier that separates consecutive iterations.\nvoid stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input,\n                      std::vector<Real>& output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const Real* __restrict const in = input.data();\n    Real* __restrict const out = output.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n    // A single pass over the grid: interior points get the stencil, boundary\n    // points are copied (same result as the original two-pass formulation).\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1) {\n            // Entire slice is boundary\n            std::memcpy(out + z * slice, in + z * slice, slice * sizeof(Real));\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // y = 0 and y = ny-1 rows are boundary\n        std::memcpy(out + z * slice, in + z * slice, nx * sizeof(Real));\n        std::memcpy(out + z * slice + (ny - 1) * nx, in + z * slice + (ny - 1) * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));\n\n        for (size_t y = 1;  // x = nx-1 boundary\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    const Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t n = grid.size();\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    bool badValue = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|| : badValue)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(g[i]) || std::isinf(g[i])) {\n            badValue = true;\n        }\n    }\n    if (badValue) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, g[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, g[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);\n            } else {\n                stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n            }\n        }\n\n#pragma omp master\n        end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    }\n\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) /  // x = nx-1 boundary\n        }\n    }\n}","new_string":"// Decomposition of the (z, y) planes into rectangular tiles. Every parallel\n// loop in this program iterates over the very same tile list with the same\n// static schedule, so a thread always works on the memory it first touched\n// (NUMA locality) and the tiles are sized so that the three y-rows a stencil\n// point needs stay in cache while the tile is swept along z.\nstruct Tiling {\n    size_t yBlock;\n    size_t zBlock;\n    size_t nyTiles;\n    size_t nzTiles;\n    size_t nTiles;\n};\n\nTiling makeTiling(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const int threads) {\n    Tiling t{};\n\n    // Keep the ~3 active y-rows of a tile (plus halo) within L2: aim at 256 KiB.\n    const size_t rowBytes = std::max<size_t>(nx, 1) * sizeof(Real);\n    size_t yb = (256 * 1024) / (3 * rowBytes);\n    yb = std::clamp<size_t>(yb, 8, std::max<size_t>(ny, 1));\n    t.yBlock = yb;\n    t.nyTiles = (ny + yb - 1) / yb;\n    if (t.nyTiles == 0) t.nyTiles = 1;\n\n    // Pick the z block so that there is enough work to balance across threads,\n    // while staying long enough that the z halo of a tile stays amortized.\n    const size_t wanted = static_cast<size_t>(std::max(threads, 1)) * 2;\n    size_t nzTiles = (wanted + t.nyTiles - 1) / t.nyTiles;\n    nzTiles = std::clamp<size_t>(nzTiles, 1, std::max<size_t>(nz / 8, 1));\n    size_t zb = (std::max<size_t>(nz, 1) + nzTiles - 1) / nzTiles;\n    zb = std::max<size_t>(zb, 1);\n    t.zBlock = zb;\n    t.nzTiles = (nz + zb - 1) / zb;\n    if (t.nzTiles == 0) t.nzTiles = 1;\n\n    t.nTiles = t.nyTiles * t.nzTiles;\n    return t;\n}\n\n// Parallel first touch + initialization. Must be called from inside a parallel\n// region.\nvoid initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.nTiles; ++tile) {\n        const size_t z0 = (tile / t.nyTiles) * t.zBlock;\n        const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + t.zBlock, nz);\n        const size_t y0 = (tile % t.nyTiles) * t.yBlock;\n        const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + t.yBlock, ny);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * slice + y * nx;\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    const size_t idx = base + x;\n                    g[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Parallel first touch of the second buffer, using the same distribution.\nvoid touchGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.nTiles; ++tile) {\n        const size_t z0 = (tile / t.nyTiles) * t.zBlock;\n        const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + t.zBlock, nz);\n        const size_t y0 = (tile % t.nyTiles) * t.yBlock;\n        const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + t.yBlock, ny);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            std::fill_n(g + z * slice + y0 * nx, (y1 - y0) * nx, Real(0));\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation. Called from inside a parallel region; the\n// `omp for` provides the barrier that separates consecutive iterations.\nvoid stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input,\n                      std::vector<Real>& output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    const Real* __restrict const in = input.data();\n    Real* __restrict const out = output.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n    if (nx == 0 || ny == 0 || nz == 0) return;  // nothing to do (all threads agree)\n    const size_t xEnd = nx - 1;\n\n    // A single pass over the grid: interior points get the stencil, boundary\n    // points are copied (same result as the original two-pass formulation).\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.nTiles; ++tile) {\n        const size_t z0 = (tile / t.nyTiles) * t.zBlock;\n        const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + t.zBlock, nz);\n        const size_t y0 = (tile % t.nyTiles) * t.yBlock;\n        const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + t.yBlock, ny);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1) {\n                // Entire slice is boundary\n                std::memcpy(out + z * slice + y0 * nx, in + z * slice + y0 * nx, (y1 - y0) * nx * sizeof(Real));\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * slice + y * nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const center = in + base;\n                Real* __restrict const dst = out + base;\n\n                if (y == 0 || y == ny - 1) {\n                    // Entire row is boundary\n                    std::memcpy(dst, center, nx * sizeof(Real));\n                    continue;\n                }\n\n                const Real* __restrict const front = center - nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const back = center + nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const bottom = center - slice;\n                const Real* __restrict const top = center + slice;\n\n                dst[0] = center[0];  // x = 0 boundary\n\n#pragma omp simd\n                for (size_t x = 1; x < xEnd; ++x) {\n                    dst[x] = (center[x] + center[x - 1] + center[x + 1] + front[x] + back[x] + bottom[x] + top[x]) / 7.0;\n                }\n\n                dst[nx - 1] = center[nx - 1]; z < nz; ++z) {\n        if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1) {\n            // Entire slice is boundary\n            std::memcpy(out + z * slice, in + z * slice, slice * sizeof(Real));\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // y = 0 and y = ny-1 rows are boundary\n        std::memcpy(out + z * slice, in + z * slice, nx * sizeof(Real));\n        std::memcpy(out + z * slice + (ny - 1) * nx, in + z * slice + (ny - 1) * nx, nx * sizeof(Real));\n\n        for (size_t y = 1; y < yEnd; ++y) {\n            const size_t base = z * slice + y * nx;\n            const Real* __restrict const center = in + base;\n            const Real* __restrict const front = in + base - nx;\n            const Real* __restrict const back = in + base + nx;\n            const Real* __restrict const bottom = in + base - slice;\n            const Real* __restrict const top = in + base + slice;\n            Real* __restrict const dst = out + base;\n\n            dst[0] = center[0];  // x = 0 boundary\n\n#pragma omp simd\n            for (size_t x = 1; x < xEnd; ++x) {\n                dst[x] = (center[x] + center[x - 1] + center[x + 1] + front[x] + back[x] + bottom[x] + top[x]) / 7.0;\n            }\n\n            dst[nx - 1] = center[nx - 1];  // x = nx-1 boundary\n        }\n    }\n}","newString":"// Decomposition of the (z, y) planes into rectangular tiles. Every parallel\n// loop in this program iterates over the very same tile list with the same\n// static schedule, so a thread always works on the memory it first touched\n// (NUMA locality) and the tiles are sized so that the three y-rows a stencil\n// point needs stay in cache while the tile is swept along z.\nstruct Tiling {\n    size_t yBlock;\n    size_t zBlock;\n    size_t nyTiles;\n    size_t nzTiles;\n    size_t nTiles;\n};\n\nTiling makeTiling(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const int threads) {\n    Tiling t{};\n\n    // Keep the ~3 active y-rows of a tile (plus halo) within L2: aim at 256 KiB.\n    const size_t rowBytes = std::max<size_t>(nx, 1) * sizeof(Real);\n    size_t yb = (256 * 1024) / (3 * rowBytes);\n    yb = std::clamp<size_t>(yb, 8, std::max<size_t>(ny, 1));\n    t.yBlock = yb;\n    t.nyTiles = (ny + yb - 1) / yb;\n    if (t.nyTiles == 0) t.nyTiles = 1;\n\n    // Pick the z block so that there is enough work to balance across threads,\n    // while staying long enough that the z halo of a tile stays amortized.\n    const size_t wanted = static_cast<size_t>(std::max(threads, 1)) * 2;\n    size_t nzTiles = (wanted + t.nyTiles - 1) / t.nyTiles;\n    nzTiles = std::clamp<size_t>(nzTiles, 1, std::max<size_t>(nz / 8, 1));\n    size_t zb = (std::max<size_t>(nz, 1) + nzTiles - 1) / nzTiles;\n    zb = std::max<size_t>(zb, 1);\n    t.zBlock = zb;\n    t.nzTiles = (nz + zb - 1) / zb;\n    if (t.nzTiles == 0) t.nzTiles = 1;\n\n    t.nTiles = t.nyTiles * t.nzTiles;\n    return t;\n}\n\n// Parallel first touch + initialization. Must be called from inside a parallel\n// region.\nvoid initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.nTiles; ++tile) {\n        const size_t z0 = (tile / t.nyTiles) * t.zBlock;\n        const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + t.zBlock, nz);\n        const size_t y0 = (tile % t.nyTiles) * t.yBlock;\n        const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + t.yBlock, ny);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * slice + y * nx;\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    const size_t idx = base + x;\n                    g[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Parallel first touch of the second buffer, using the same distribution.\nvoid touchGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.nTiles; ++tile) {\n        const size_t z0 = (tile / t.nyTiles) * t.zBlock;\n        const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + t.zBlock, nz);\n        const size_t y0 = (tile % t.nyTiles) * t.yBlock;\n        const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + t.yBlock, ny);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            std::fill_n(g + z * slice + y0 * nx, (y1 - y0) * nx, Real(0));\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation. Called from inside a parallel region; the\n// `omp for` provides the barrier that separates consecutive iterations.\nvoid stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input,\n                      std::vector<Real>& output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    const Real* __restrict const in = input.data();\n    Real* __restrict const out = output.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n    if (nx == 0 || ny == 0 || nz == 0) return;  // nothing to do (all threads agree)\n    const size_t xEnd = nx - 1;\n\n    // A single pass over the grid: interior points get the stencil, boundary\n    // points are copied (same result as the original two-pass formulation).\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.nTiles; ++tile) {\n        const size_t z0 = (tile / t.nyTiles) * t.zBlock;\n        const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + t.zBlock, nz);\n        const size_t y0 = (tile % t.nyTiles) * t.yBlock;\n        const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + t.yBlock, ny);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1) {\n                // Entire slice is boundary\n                std::memcpy(out + z * slice + y0 * nx, in + z * slice + y0 * nx, (y1 - y0) * nx * sizeof(Real));\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * slice + y * nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const center = in + base;\n                Real* __restrict const dst = out + base;\n\n                if (y == 0 || y == ny - 1) {\n                    // Entire row is boundary\n                    std::memcpy(dst, center, nx * sizeof(Real));\n                    continue;\n                }\n\n                const Real* __restrict const front = center - nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const back = center + nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const bottom = center - slice;\n                const Real* __restrict const top = center + slice;\n\n                dst[0] = center[0];  // x = 0 boundary\n\n#pragma omp simd\n                for (size_t x = 1; x < xEnd; ++x) {\n                    dst[x] = (center[x] + center[x - 1] + center[x + 1] + front[x] + back[x] + bottom[x] + top[x]) / 7.0;\n                }\n\n                dst[nx - 1] = center[nx - 1];  // x = nx-1 boundary\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}","originalFile":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdint>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <omp.h>\n#include <sys/mman.h>\n#include <unistd.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nusing Real = double;\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Drop the physical pages backing a freshly allocated (zero-initialized) buffer.\n// The contents stay all-zero, but the pages are re-faulted by whichever thread\n// touches them first, so the parallel initialization below determines the NUMA\n// placement of the grids instead of the thread that ran the allocation.\nvoid releasePages(std::vector<Real>& grid) {\n    const size_t pageSize = static_cast<size_t>(sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE));\n    const uintptr_t begin = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(grid.data());\n    const uintptr_t end = begin + grid.size() * sizeof(Real);\n    const uintptr_t alignedBegin = (begin + pageSize - 1) & ~static_cast<uintptr_t>(pageSize - 1);\n    const uintptr_t alignedEnd = end & ~static_cast<uintptr_t>(pageSize - 1);\n    if (alignedEnd > alignedBegin) {\n        madvise(reinterpret_cast<void*>(alignedBegin), alignedEnd - alignedBegin, MADV_DONTNEED);\n    }\n}\n\n// Parallel first touch + initialization. Must be called from inside a parallel\n// region; the static schedule over z matches the one used by the stencil, so\n// every thread initializes exactly the slices it will later compute on.\nvoid initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t base = z * slice + y * nx;\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = base + x;\n                g[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Parallel first touch of the second buffer, using the same distribution.\nvoid touchGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        std::fill_n(g + z * slice, slice, Real(0));\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation. constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Drop the physical pages backing a freshly allocated (zero-initialized) buffer.\n// The contents stay all-zero, but the pages are re-faulted by whichever thread\n// touches them first, so the parallel initialization below determines the NUMA\n// placement of the grids instead of the thread that ran the allocation.\nvoid releasePages(std::vector<Real>& grid) {\n    const size_t pageSize = static_cast<size_t>(sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE));\n    const uintptr_t begin = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(grid.data());\n    const uintptr_t end = begin + grid.size() * sizeof(Real);\n    const uintptr_t alignedBegin = (begin + pageSize - 1) & ~static_cast<uintptr_t>(pageSize - 1);\n    const uintptr_t alignedEnd = end & ~static_cast<uintptr_t>(pageSize - 1);\n    if (alignedEnd > alignedBegin) {\n        madvise(reinterpret_cast<void*>(alignedBegin), alignedEnd - alignedBegin, MADV_DONTNEED);\n    }\n}\n\n// Decomposition of the (z, y) planes into rectangular tiles. Every parallel\n// loop in this program iterates over the very same tile list with the same\n// static schedule, so a thread always works on the memory it first touched\n// (NUMA locality) and the tiles are sized so that the three y-rows a stencil\n// point needs stay in cache while the tile is swept along z.\nstruct Tiling {\n    size_t yBlock;\n    size_t zBlock;\n    size_t nyTiles;\n    size_t nzTiles;\n    size_t nTiles;\n};\n\nTiling makeTiling(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const int threads) {\n    Tiling t{};\n\n    // Keep the ~3 active y-rows of a tile (plus halo) within L2: aim at 256 KiB.\n    const size_t rowBytes = std::max<size_t>(nx, 1) * sizeof(Real);\n    size_t yb = (256 * 1024) / (3 * rowBytes);\n    yb = std::clamp<size_t>(yb, 8, std::max<size_t>(ny, 1));\n    t.yBlock = yb;\n    t.nyTiles = (ny + yb - 1) / yb;\n    if (t.nyTiles == 0) t.nyTiles = 1;\n\n    // Pick the z block so that there is enough work to balance across threads,\n    // while staying long enough that the z halo of a tile stays amortized.\n    const size_t wanted = static_cast<size_t>(std::max(threads, 1)) * 2;\n    size_t nzTiles = (wanted + t.nyTiles - 1) / t.nyTiles;\n    nzTiles = std::clamp<size_t>(nzTiles, 1, std::max<size_t>(nz / 8, 1));\n    size_t zb = (std::max<size_t>(nz, 1) + nzTiles - 1) / nzTiles;\n    zb = std::max<size_t>(zb, 1);\n    t.zBlock = zb;\n    t.nzTiles = (nz + zb - 1) / zb;\n    if (t.nzTiles == 0) t.nzTiles = 1;\n\n    t.nTiles = t.nyTiles * t.nzTiles;\n    return t;\n}\n\n// Parallel first touch + initialization. Must be called from inside a parallel\n// region.\nvoid initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.nTiles; ++tile) {\n        const size_t z0 = (tile / t.nyTiles) * t.zBlock;\n        const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + t.zBlock, nz);\n        const size_t y0 = (tile % t.nyTiles) * t.yBlock;\n        const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + t.yBlock, ny);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * slice + y * nx;\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    const size_t idx = base + x;\n                    g[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Parallel first touch of the second buffer, using the same distribution.\nvoid touchGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.nTiles; ++tile) {\n        const size_t z0 = (tile / t.nyTiles) * t.zBlock;\n        const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + t.zBlock, nz);\n        const size_t y0 = (tile % t.nyTiles) * t.yBlock;\n        const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + t.yBlock, ny);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            std::fill_n(g + z * slice + y0 * nx, (y1 - y0) * nx, Real(0));\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation. Called from inside a parallel region; the\n// `omp for` provides the barrier that separates consecutive iterations.\nvoid stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input,\n                      std::vector<Real>& output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    const Real* __restrict const in = input.data();\n    Real* __restrict const out = output.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n    if (nx == 0 || ny == 0 || nz == 0) return; 1) & ~static_cast<uintptr_t>(pageSize - 1);\n    const uintptr_t alignedEnd = end & ~static_cast<uintptr_t>(pageSize - 1);\n    if (alignedEnd > alignedBegin) {\n        madvise(reinterpret_cast<void*>(alignedBegin), alignedEnd - alignedBegin, MADV_DONTNEED);\n    }\n}\n\n// Decomposition of the (z, y) planes into rectangular tiles. Every parallel\n// loop in this program iterates over the very same tile list with the same\n// static schedule, so a thread always works on the memory it first touched\n// (NUMA locality) and the tiles are sized so that the three y-rows a stencil\n// point needs stay in cache while the tile is swept along z.\nstruct Tiling {\n    size_t yBlock;\n    size_t zBlock;\n    size_t nyTiles;\n    size_t nzTiles;\n    size_t nTiles;\n};\n\nTiling makeTiling(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const int threads) {\n    Tiling t{};\n\n    // Keep the ~3 active y-rows of a tile (plus halo) within L2: aim at 256 KiB.\n    const size_t rowBytes = std::max<size_t>(nx, 1) * sizeof(Real);\n    size_t yb = (256 * 1024) / (3 * rowBytes);\n    yb = std::clamp<size_t>(yb, 8, std::max<size_t>(ny, 1));\n    t.yBlock = yb;\n    t.nyTiles = (ny + yb - 1) / yb;\n    if (t.nyTiles == 0) t.nyTiles = 1;\n\n    // Pick the z block so that there is enough work to balance across threads,\n    // while staying long enough that the z halo of a tile stays amortized.\n    const size_t wanted = static_cast<size_t>(std::max(threads, 1)) * 2;\n    size_t nzTiles = (wanted + t.nyTiles - 1) / t.nyTiles;\n    nzTiles = std::clamp<size_t>(nzTiles, 1, std::max<size_t>(nz / 8, 1));\n    size_t zb = (std::max<size_t>(nz, 1) + nzTiles - 1) / nzTiles;\n    zb = std::max<size_t>(zb, 1);\n    t.zBlock = zb;\n    t.nzTiles = (nz + zb - 1) / zb;\n    if (t.nzTiles == 0) t.nzTiles = 1;\n\n    t.nTiles = t.nyTiles * t.nzTiles;\n    return t;\n}\n\n// Parallel first touch + initialization. Must be called from inside a parallel\n// region.\nvoid initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.nTiles; ++tile) {\n        const size_t z0 = (tile / t.nyTiles) * t.zBlock;\n        const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + t.zBlock, nz);\n        const size_t y0 = (tile % t.nyTiles) * t.yBlock;\n        const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + t.yBlock, ny);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * slice + y * nx;\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    const size_t idx = base + x;\n                    g[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Parallel first touch of the second buffer, using the same distribution.\nvoid touchGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.nTiles; ++tile) {\n        const size_t z0 = (tile / t.nyTiles) * t.zBlock;\n        const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + t.zBlock, nz);\n        const size_t y0 = (tile % t.nyTiles) * t.yBlock;\n        const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + t.yBlock, ny);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            std::fill_n(g + z * slice + y0 * nx, (y1 - y0) * nx, Real(0));\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation. Called from inside a parallel region; the\n// `omp for` provides the barrier that separates consecutive iterations.\nvoid stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input,\n                      std::vector<Real>& output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    const Real* __restrict const in = input.data();\n    Real* __restrict const out = output.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n    if (nx == 0 || ny == 0 || nz == 0) return;  // nothing to do (all threads agree)\n    const size_t xEnd = nx - 1;\n\n    // A single pass over the grid: interior points get the stencil, boundary\n    // points are copied (same result as the original two-pass formulation).\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.nTiles; ++tile) {\n        const size_t z0 = (tile / t.nyTiles) * t.zBlock;\n        const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + t.zBlock, nz);\n        const size_t y0 = (tile % t.nyTiles) * t.yBlock;\n        const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + t.yBlock, ny);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1) {\n                // Entire slice is boundary\n                std::memcpy(out + z * slice + y0 * nx, in + z * slice + y0 * nx, (y1 - y0) * nx * sizeof(Real));\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * slice + y * nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const center = in + base;\n                Real* __restrict const dst = out + base;\n\n                if (y == 0 || y == ny - 1) {\n                    // Entire row is boundary\n                    std::memcpy(dst, center, nx * sizeof(Real));\n                    continue;\n                }\n\n                const Real* __restrict const front = center - nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const back = center + nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const bottom = center - slice;\n                const Real* __restrict const top = center + slice;\n\n                dst[0] = center[0];  // x = 0 boundary\n\n#pragma omp simd\n                for (size_t x = 1; x < xEnd; ++x) {\n                    dst[x] = (center[x] + center[x - 1] + center[x + 1] + front[x] + back[x] + bottom[x] + top[x]) / 7.0;\n                }\n\n                dst[nx - 1] = center[nx - 1];  // x = nx-1 boundary\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    const Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t n = grid.size();\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    bool badValue = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|| : badValue)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(g[i]) || std::isinf(g[i])) {\n            badValue = true;\n        }\n    }\n    if (badValue) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, g[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, g[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate grids (double buffering)\n    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);\n    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);\n\n    // Undo the serial first touch performed by the allocation, so the parallel\n    // initialization below distributes the pages across the NUMA domains.\n    releasePages(grid1);\n    releasePages(grid2);\n\n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");\n    printf(\"Running stencil initialization and all iterations: threads keep\n    // working on the data they first touched, and there is no repeated fork/join.\n#pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f 1) & ~static_cast<uintptr_t>(pageSize - 1);\n    const uintptr_t alignedEnd = end & ~static_cast<uintptr_t>(pageSize - 1);\n    if (alignedEnd > alignedBegin) {\n        madvise(reinterpret_cast<void*>(alignedBegin), alignedEnd - alignedBegin, MADV_DONTNEED);\n    }\n}\n\n// Decomposition of the (z, y) planes into rectangular tiles. Every parallel\n// loop in this program iterates over the very same tile list with the same\n// static schedule, so a thread always works on the memory it first touched\n// (NUMA locality) and the tiles are sized so that the three y-rows a stencil\n// point needs stay in cache while the tile is swept along z.\nstruct Tiling {\n    size_t yBlock;\n    size_t zBlock;\n    size_t nyTiles;\n    size_t nzTiles;\n    size_t nTiles;\n};\n\nTiling makeTiling(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const int threads) {\n    Tiling t{};\n\n    // Keep the ~3 active y-rows of a tile (plus halo) within L2: aim at 256 KiB.\n    const size_t rowBytes = std::max<size_t>(nx, 1) * sizeof(Real);\n    size_t yb = (256 * 1024) / (3 * rowBytes);\n    yb = std::clamp<size_t>(yb, 8, std::max<size_t>(ny, 1));\n    t.yBlock = yb;\n    t.nyTiles = (ny + yb - 1) / yb;\n    if (t.nyTiles == 0) t.nyTiles = 1;\n\n    // Pick the z block so that there is enough work to balance across threads,\n    // while staying long enough that the z halo of a tile stays amortized.\n    const size_t wanted = static_cast<size_t>(std::max(threads, 1)) * 2;\n    size_t nzTiles = (wanted + t.nyTiles - 1) / t.nyTiles;\n    nzTiles = std::clamp<size_t>(nzTiles, 1, std::max<size_t>(nz / 8, 1));\n    size_t zb = (std::max<size_t>(nz, 1) + nzTiles - 1) / nzTiles;\n    zb = std::max<size_t>(zb, 1);\n    t.zBlock = zb;\n    t.nzTiles = (nz + zb - 1) / zb;\n    if (t.nzTiles == 0) t.nzTiles = 1;\n\n    t.nTiles = t.nyTiles * t.nzTiles;\n    return t;\n}\n\n// Parallel first touch + initialization. Must be called from inside a parallel\n// region.\nvoid initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.nTiles; ++tile) {\n        const size_t z0 = (tile / t.nyTiles) * t.zBlock;\n        const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + t.zBlock, nz);\n        const size_t y0 = (tile % t.nyTiles) * t.yBlock;\n        const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + t.yBlock, ny);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * slice + y * nx;\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    const size_t idx = base + x;\n                    g[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Parallel first touch of the second buffer, using the same distribution.\nvoid touchGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.nTiles; ++tile) {\n        const size_t z0 = (tile / t.nyTiles) * t.zBlock;\n        const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + t.zBlock, nz);\n        const size_t y0 = (tile % t.nyTiles) * t.yBlock;\n        const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + t.yBlock, ny);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            std::fill_n(g + z * slice + y0 * nx, (y1 - y0) * nx, Real(0));\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation. Called from inside a parallel region; the\n// `omp for` provides the barrier that separates consecutive iterations.\nvoid stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input,\n                      std::vector<Real>& output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    const Real* __restrict const in = input.data();\n    Real* __restrict const out = output.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n    if (nx == 0 || ny == 0 || nz == 0) return;  // nothing to do (all threads agree)\n    const size_t xEnd = nx - 1;\n\n    // A single pass over the grid: interior points get the stencil, boundary\n    // points are copied (same result as the original two-pass formulation).\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.nTiles; ++tile) {\n        const size_t z0 = (tile / t.nyTiles) * t.zBlock;\n        const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + t.zBlock, nz);\n        const size_t y0 = (tile % t.nyTiles) * t.yBlock;\n        const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + t.yBlock, ny);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1) {\n                // Entire slice is boundary\n                std::memcpy(out + z * slice + y0 * nx, in + z * slice + y0 * nx, (y1 - y0) * nx * sizeof(Real));\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * slice + y * nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const center = in + base;\n                Real* __restrict const dst = out + base;\n\n                if (y == 0 || y == ny - 1) {\n                    // Entire row is boundary\n                    std::memcpy(dst, center, nx * sizeof(Real));\n                    continue;\n                }\n\n                const Real* __restrict const front = center - nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const back = center + nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const bottom = center - slice;\n                const Real* __restrict const top = center + slice;\n\n                dst[0] = center[0];  // x = 0 boundary\n\n#pragma omp simd\n                for (size_t x = 1; x < xEnd; ++x) {\n                    dst[x] = (center[x] + center[x - 1] + center[x + 1] + front[x] + back[x] + bottom[x] + top[x]) / 7.0;\n                }\n\n                dst[nx - 1] = center[nx - 1];  // x = nx-1 boundary\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    const Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t n = grid.size();\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    bool badValue = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|| : badValue)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(g[i]) || std::isinf(g[i])) {\n            badValue = true;\n        }\n    }\n    if (badValue) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, g[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, g[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate grids (double buffering)\n    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);\n    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);\n\n    // Undo the serial first touch performed by the allocation, so the parallel\n    // initialization below distributes the pages across the NUMA domains.\n    releasePages(grid1);\n    releasePages(grid2);\n\n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");\n    printf(\"Running stencil initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz, tiling);\n        touchGrid(grid2, nx, ny, nz, tiling);\n\n#pragma omp master\n        start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n#pragma omp barrier\n\n stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz, tiling);\n            }\n        }\n\n#pragma omp master\n        end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    }\n\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f Every parallel\n// loop in this program iterates over the very same tile list with the same\n// static schedule, so a thread always works on the memory it first touched\n// (NUMA locality) and the tiles are sized so that the three y-rows a stencil\n// point needs stay in cache while the tile is swept along z.\nstruct Tiling {\n    size_t yBlock;\n    size_t zBlock;\n    size_t nyTiles;\n    size_t nzTiles;\n    size_t nTiles;\n};\n\nTiling makeTiling(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const int threads) {\n    Tiling t{};\n\n    // Keep the ~3 active y-rows of a tile (plus halo) within L2: aim at 128 KiB.\n    const size_t rowBytes = std::max<size_t>(nx, 1) * sizeof(Real);\n    size_t yb = (128 * 1024) / (3 * rowBytes);\n    yb = std::clamp<size_t>(yb, 8, std::max<size_t>(ny, 1));\n    t.yBlock = yb;\n    t.nyTiles = (ny + yb - 1) / yb;\n    if (t.nyTiles == 0) t.nyTiles = 1;\n\n    // Pick the z block so that there is enough work to balance across threads,\n    // while staying long enough that the z halo of a tile stays amortized.\n    const size_t wanted = static_cast<size_t>(std::max(threads, 1)) * 2;\n    size_t nzTiles = (wanted + t.nyTiles - 1) / t.nyTiles;\n    nzTiles = std::clamp<size_t>(nzTiles, 1, std::max<size_t>(nz / 8, 1));\n    size_t zb = (std::max<size_t>(nz, 1) + nzTiles - 1) / nzTiles;\n    zb = std::max<size_t>(zb, 1);\n    t.zBlock = zb;\n    t.nzTiles = (nz + zb - 1) / zb;\n    if (t.nzTiles == 0) t.nzTiles = 1;\n\n    t.nTiles = t.nyTiles * t.nzTiles;\n    return t;\n}\n\n// Parallel first touch + initialization. Must be called from inside a parallel\n// region.\nvoid initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.nTiles; ++tile) {\n        const size_t z0 = (tile / t.nyTiles) * t.zBlock;\n        const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + t.zBlock, nz);\n        const size_t y0 = (tile % t.nyTiles) * t.yBlock;\n        const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + t.yBlock, ny);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * slice + y * nx;\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    const size_t idx = base + x;\n                    g[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Parallel first touch of the second buffer, using the same distribution.\nvoid touchGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.nTiles; ++tile) {\n        const size_t z0 = (tile / t.nyTiles) * t.zBlock;\n        const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + t.zBlock, nz);\n        const size_t y0 = (tile % t.nyTiles) * t.yBlock;\n        const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + t.yBlock, ny);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            std::fill_n(g + z * slice + y0 * nx, (y1 - y0) * nx, Real(0));\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation. Called from inside a parallel region; the\n// `omp for` provides the barrier that separates consecutive iterations.\nvoid stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input,\n                      std::vector<Real>& output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    const Real* __restrict const in = input.data();\n    Real* __restrict const out = output.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n    if (nx == 0 || ny == 0 || nz == 0) return;  // nothing to do (all threads agree)\n    const size_t xEnd = nx - 1;\n\n    // A single pass over the grid: interior points get the stencil, boundary\n    // points are copied (same result as the original two-pass formulation).\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.nTiles; ++tile) {\n        const size_t z0 = (tile / t.nyTiles) * t.zBlock;\n        const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + t.zBlock, nz);\n        const size_t y0 = (tile % t.nyTiles) * t.yBlock;\n        const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + t.yBlock, ny);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1) {\n                // Entire slice is boundary\n                std::memcpy(out + z * slice + y0 * nx, in + z * slice + y0 * nx, (y1 - y0) * nx * sizeof(Real));\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * slice + y * nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const center = in + base;\n                Real* __restrict const dst = out + base;\n\n                if (y == 0 || y == ny - 1) {\n                    // Entire row is boundary\n                    std::memcpy(dst, center, nx * sizeof(Real));\n                    continue;\n                }\n\n                const Real* __restrict const front = center - nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const back = center + nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const bottom = center - slice;\n                const Real* __restrict const top = center + slice;\n\n                dst[0] = center[0];  // x = 0 boundary\n\n#pragma omp simd\n                for (size_t x = 1; x < xEnd; ++x) {\n                    dst[x] = (center[x] + center[x - 1] + center[x + 1] + front[x] + back[x] + bottom[x] + top[x]) / 7.0;\n                }\n\n                dst[nx - 1] = center[nx - 1];  // x = nx-1 boundary\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    const Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t n = grid.size();\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    bool badValue = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|| : badValue)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(g[i]) || std::isinf(g[i])) {\n            badValue = true;\n        }\n    }\n    if (badValue) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, g[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, g[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate grids (double buffering)\n    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);\n    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);\n\n    // Undo the serial first touch performed by the allocation, so the parallel\n    // initialization below distributes the pages across the NUMA domains.\n    releasePages(grid1);\n    releasePages(grid2);\n\n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");\n    printf(\"Running stencil computation...\\n\");\n\n    std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::time_point start, end;\n\n    const Tiling tiling = makeTiling(nx, ny, nz, omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    // One parallel region covers initialization and all iterations: threads keep\n    // working on the data they first touched, and there is no repeated fork/join.\n#pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz, tiling);\n        touchGrid(grid2, nx, ny, nz, tiling);\n\n#pragma omp master\n        start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n#pragma omp barrier\n\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n            if (iter % 2 == 0) {\n                stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz, tiling);\n            } else {\n                stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz, tiling);\n            }\n        }\n\n#pragma omp master\n        end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    }\n\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(finalGrid, \"Grid\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n Unless the user asked for a specific thread count, use one\n// thread per physical core.\nvoid selectThreadCount() {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") != nullptr) return;\n    const int available = omp_get_max_threads();\n    const int cores = available / threadsPerCore();\n    if (cores >= 1 && cores < available) {\n        omp_set_num_threads(cores);\n    }\n}\n\n// Decomposition of the (z, y) planes into rectangular tiles.","originalFile":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdint>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <omp.h>\n#include <sys/mman.h>\n#include <unistd.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nusing Real = double;\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Drop the physical pages backing a freshly allocated (zero-initialized) buffer.\n// The contents stay all-zero, but the pages are re-faulted by whichever thread\n// touches them first, so the parallel initialization below determines the NUMA\n// placement of the grids instead of the thread that ran the allocation.\nvoid releasePages(std::vector<Real>& grid) {\n    const size_t pageSize = static_cast<size_t>(sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE));\n    const uintptr_t begin = reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(grid.data());\n    const uintptr_t end = begin + grid.size() * sizeof(Real);\n    const uintptr_t alignedBegin = (begin + pageSize - 1) & ~static_cast<uintptr_t>(pageSize - 1);\n    const uintptr_t alignedEnd = end & ~static_cast<uintptr_t>(pageSize - 1);\n    if (alignedEnd > alignedBegin) {\n        madvise(reinterpret_cast<void*>(alignedBegin), alignedEnd - alignedBegin, MADV_DONTNEED);\n    }\n}\n\n// Decomposition of the (z, y) planes into rectangular tiles. Every parallel\n// loop in this program iterates over the very same tile list with the same\n// static schedule, so a thread always works on the memory it first touched\n// (NUMA locality) and the tiles are sized so that the three y-rows a stencil\n// point needs stay in cache while the tile is swept along z.\nstruct Tiling {\n    size_t yBlock;\n    size_t zBlock;\n    size_t nyTiles;\n    size_t nzTiles;\n    size_t nTiles;\n};\n\nTiling makeTiling(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const int threads) {\n    Tiling t{};\n\n    // Keep the ~3 active y-rows of a tile (plus halo) within L2: aim at 128 KiB.\n    const size_t rowBytes = std::max<size_t>(nx, 1) * sizeof(Real);\n    size_t yb = (128 * 1024) / (3 * rowBytes);\n    yb = std::clamp<size_t>(yb, 8, std::max<size_t>(ny, 1));\n    t.yBlock = yb;\n    t.nyTiles = (ny + yb - 1) / yb;\n    if (t.nyTiles == 0) t.nyTiles = 1;\n\n    // Pick the z block so that there is enough work to balance across threads,\n    // while staying long enough that the z halo of a tile stays amortized.\n    const size_t wanted = static_cast<size_t>(std::max(threads, 1)) * 3;\n    size_t nzTiles = (wanted + t.nyTiles - 1) / t.nyTiles;\n    nzTiles = std::clamp<size_t>(nzTiles, 1, std::max<size_t>(nz / 8, 1));\n    size_t zb = (std::max<size_t>(nz, 1) + nzTiles - 1) / nzTiles;\n    zb = std::max<size_t>(zb, 1);\n    t.zBlock = zb;\n    t.nzTiles = (nz + zb - 1) / zb;\n    if (t.nzTiles == 0) t.nzTiles = 1;\n\n    t.nTiles = t.nyTiles * t.nzTiles;\n    return t;\n}\n\n// Parallel first touch + initialization. Must be called from inside a parallel\n// region.\nvoid initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.nTiles; ++tile) {\n        const size_t z0 = (tile / t.nyTiles) * t.zBlock;\n        const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + t.zBlock, nz);\n        const size_t y0 = (tile % t.nyTiles) * t.yBlock;\n        const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + t.yBlock, ny);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * slice + y * nx;\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    const size_t idx = base + x;\n                    g[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Parallel first touch of the second buffer, using the same distribution.\nvoid touchGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.nTiles; ++tile) {\n        const size_t z0 = (tile / t.nyTiles) * t.zBlock;\n        const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + t.zBlock, nz);\n        const size_t y0 = (tile % t.nyTiles) * t.yBlock;\n        const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + t.yBlock, ny);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            std::fill_n(g + z * slice + y0 * nx, (y1 - y0) * nx, Real(0));\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation. Called from inside a parallel region; the\n// `omp for` provides the barrier that separates consecutive iterations.\nvoid stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input,\n                      std::vector<Real>& output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz, const Tiling& t) {\n    const Real* __restrict const in = input.data();\n    Real* __restrict const out = output.data();\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n    if (nx == 0 || ny == 0 || nz == 0) return;  // nothing to do (all threads agree)\n    const size_t xEnd = nx - 1;\n\n    // A single pass over the grid: interior points get the stencil, boundary\n    // points are copied (same result as the original two-pass formulation).\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t tile = 0; tile < t.nTiles; ++tile) {\n        const size_t z0 = (tile / t.nyTiles) * t.zBlock;\n        const size_t z1 = std::min(z0 + t.zBlock, nz);\n        const size_t y0 = (tile % t.nyTiles) * t.yBlock;\n        const size_t y1 = std::min(y0 + t.yBlock, ny);\n\n        for (size_t z = z0; z < z1; ++z) {\n            if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1) {\n                // Entire slice is boundary\n                std::memcpy(out + z * slice + y0 * nx, in + z * slice + y0 * nx, (y1 - y0) * nx * sizeof(Real));\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            for (size_t y = y0; y < y1; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = z * slice + y * nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const center = in + base;\n                Real* __restrict const dst = out + base;\n\n                if (y == 0 || y == ny - 1) {\n                    // Entire row is boundary\n                    std::memcpy(dst, center, nx * sizeof(Real));\n                    continue;\n                }\n\n                const Real* __restrict const front = center - nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const back = center + nx;\n                const Real* __restrict const bottom = center - slice;\n                const Real* __restrict const top = center + slice;\n\n                dst[0] = center[0];  // x = 0 boundary\n\n#pragma omp simd\n                for (size_t x = 1; x < xEnd; ++x) {\n                    dst[x] = (center[x] + center[x - 1] + center[x + 1] + front[x] + back[x] + bottom[x] + top[x]) / 7.0;\n                }\n\n                dst[nx - 1] = center[nx - 1];  // x = nx-1 boundary\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    const Real* __restrict const g = grid.data();\n    const size_t n = grid.size();\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    bool badValue = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|| : badValue)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        if (std::isnan(g[i]) || std::isinf(g[i])) {\n            badValue = true;\n        }\n    }\n    if (badValue) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, g[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, g[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate grids (double buffering)\n    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);\n    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);\n\n    // Undo the serial first touch performed by the allocation, so the parallel\n    // initialization below distributes the pages across the NUMA domains.\n    releasePages(grid1);\n    releasePages(grid2);\n\n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");\n    printf(\"Running stencil computation...\\n\");\n\n    std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::time_point start, end;\n\n    const Tiling tiling = makeTiling(nx, ny, nz, omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    // One parallel region covers initialization and all iterations: threads keep\n    // working on the data they first touched, and there is no repeated fork/join.\n#pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz, tiling);\n        touchGrid(grid2, nx, ny, nz, tiling);\n\n#pragma omp master\n        start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n#pragma omp barrier\n\n stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz, tiling);\n            }\n        }\n\n#pragma omp master\n        end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    }\n\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f
